| `cancel_projects_locations_agents_tasks` | `POST /v1beta1/{name+}:cancel` | Deprecated: use `CancelTaskLro` instead. This method keeps the same cancellation semantics but returns `Task` directly, which forecloses the future cascade behaviour on recurring parents that only an `Operation` return type can express. Cancels a task, out-of-band by id. Drains any in-progress work and moves the task to CANCELLED. Use this when there is no active stream (e.g. a scheduled or background task) or when a caller other than the streaming client cancels. A task may also be cancelled in-band via the bidi cancel_task end_request; the two paths are equivalent. Cancellation semantics: * Idempotent: cancelling an already-terminal task is a no-op that returns the current Task; concurrent/duplicate cancels collapse to one CANCELLED. * First terminal state wins: once the task is CANCELLED/COMPLETED/FAILED, later cancels have no effect. * Cancel takes precedence over complete_task: a cancel arriving while (or after) a client half-closes with complete_task still drives the task to CANCELLED rather than letting it finish. |

| `check_trial_early_stopping_state_projects_locations_studies_trials` | `POST /v1beta1/{trialName+}:checkTrialEarlyStoppingState` | Checks whether a Trial should stop or not. Returns a long-running operation. When the operation is successful, it will contain a CheckTrialEarlyStoppingStateResponse. |
| `check_vertex_express_eligibility` | `GET /v1beta1/vertexExpress/Eligibility:check` | Checks whether the user is eligible to sign up for Vertex AI Express Mode. |
| `clear_training_data_projects_locations_monitored_agents` | `POST /v1beta1/{name+}:clearTrainingData` | Clears (purges) the trained model artifacts derived from a MonitoredAgent's data. The purged model is rebuilt by the next scheduled training run. To clear every MonitoredAgent in a location's scope, use the `-` wildcard agent segment for the name field: `projects/{project}/locations/{location}/monitoredAgents/-` This is the on-demand data-deletion path for customers who have deleted the underlying logs / observability data a model was trained on. It deletes only the derived model artifacts in the tenant project (it does NOT delete the scope, tenant project, or monitoring). To stop monitoring an agent entirely, disable it via DisableMonitoredAgent. See go/aad-clear-training-data. |
| `compact_projects_locations_publishers_v1_responses` | `POST /v1beta1/{endpoint+}/v1/responses/compact` | Forwards arbitrary HTTP requests for both streaming and non-streaming cases. To use this method, invoke_route_prefix must be set to allow the paths that will be specified in the request. |
| `compact_projects_locations_reasoning_engines_sessions` | `POST /v1beta1/{name+}:compact` | Compacts the event history of a given Session, which may run an LLM summarization call and rewrite the full event history. Compaction is a storage-side rewrite that can apply a stackable pipeline of rules (event-horizon preservation, tool-response truncation, thought stripping, and LLM summarization etc.) |

| `create_projects_locations_evaluation_sets_revisions` | `POST /v1beta1/{parent+}/revisions` | Creates an Evaluation Set Revision: an immutable snapshot of the Evaluation Set and its Evaluation Items. Each entry in the set's `evaluation_items` is replaced by an Evaluation Item Revision of that item. Every Evaluation Item in the set must be of type `HARBOR_TASK`. It must not contain duplicate items. Currently this always creates a new Evaluation Item Revision for every item. Clients must not rely on this: a future version may reuse an item's latest Evaluation Item Revision when the item has not changed since that revision was created. |

| `download_artifact_projects_locations_agents_tasks` | `GET /v1beta1/{task+}:downloadArtifact` | Downloads a file from a task's workspace as a stream of chunks. A server-streaming RPC. The response body is the file's raw bytes, with the file's own media type as the `Content-Type`. At most 1 GiB. Like ListTaskArtifacts, reads from the running sandbox when there is one, so a file the agent has just written is returned even before it reaches storage. |
| `download_media` | `GET /v1beta1/{name+}:download` | Downloads a file. The response body is the file's bytes, with the file's own media type as the `Content-Type` and a `Content-Disposition` that names the file. Media is the default and only representation, so no `alt` parameter is needed. A preview sets `processing_config` and gets a rendition of the file in place of the file, such as a PDF of a .pptx: the response body is the rendition, with its own media type as the `Content-Type` and a `Content-Disposition` name that ends in its extension. Without `processing_config` the response is always the stored file. |

| `import_feature_values_projects_locations_featurestores_entity_types` | `POST /v1beta1/{entityType+}:importFeatureValues` | Imports Feature values into the Featurestore from a source storage. The progress of the import is tracked by the returned operation. The imported features are guaranteed to be visible to subsequent read operations after the operation is marked as successfully done. If an import operation fails, the Feature values returned from reads and exports may be inconsistent. If consistency is required, the caller must retry the same import request again and wait till the new operation returned is marked as successfully done. There are also scenarios where the caller can cause inconsistency. - Source data for import contains multiple distinct Feature values for the same entity ID and timestamp. - Source is modified during an import. This includes adding, updating, or removing source data and/or metadata. Examples of updating metadata include but are not limited to changing storage location, storage class, or retention policy. - Online serving cluster is under-provisioned. |

By default only the immediate children of `path` are returned; set `recursive` to descend into subdirectories. Entries are returned in breadth-first order. Which one served a request is not visible to the caller. |

| `list_projects_locations` (+ `_pages`, `_items`) | `GET /v1beta1/{name+}/locations` | Lists information about the supported locations for this service. This method lists locations based on the resource scope provided in the ListLocationsRequest.name field: * **Global locations**: If `name` is empty, the method lists the public locations available to all projects. * **Project-specific locations**: If `name` follows the format `projects/{project}`, the method lists locations visible to that specific project. This includes public, private, or other project-specific locations enabled for the project. For gRPC and client library implementations, the resource name is passed as the `name` field. For direct service calls, the resource name is incorporated into the request path based on the specific service implementation and version. |

An agent belongs to the end user recorded as its owner when it was created, so the response holds that caller's agents and no others. It is empty for a caller that is not an end user, and an agent with no recorded owner is listed for nobody. |

| `preview_projects_locations_agents_tasks_artifacts` | `GET /v1beta1/{parent+}/artifacts:preview` | Returns a file converted for a preview, such as a PDF of a .pptx. A server-streaming RPC. The response body is the converted file's bytes, with its media type as the `Content-Type`. The server converts the file as it is stored now, on every call, and stores nothing. A PDF asked for as a PDF is returned as stored. Fails with `INVALID_ARGUMENT` or `FAILED_PRECONDITION` when the file cannot be converted (an unsupported format, too large, or unreadable), with `DEADLINE_EXCEEDED` when the conversion takes too long, and with `UNAVAILABLE` when conversion is busy or not available; the last two may succeed on a retry. |
| `preview_projects_locations_collaborative_projects_artifacts` | `GET /v1beta1/{parent+}/artifacts:preview` | Returns a file converted for a preview, such as a PDF of a .pptx. A server-streaming RPC. The response body is the converted file's bytes, with its media type as the `Content-Type`. The server converts the file as it is stored now, on every call, and stores nothing. A PDF asked for as a PDF is returned as stored. Fails with `INVALID_ARGUMENT` or `FAILED_PRECONDITION` when the file cannot be converted (an unsupported format, too large, or unreadable), with `DEADLINE_EXCEEDED` when the conversion takes too long, and with `UNAVAILABLE` when conversion is busy or not available; the last two may succeed on a retry. |

| `stream_projects_locations_agents_tasks` | `GET /v1beta1/{name+}:stream` | Read-only server-stream of a task's execution by id: trajectory steps and status changes, resumable from a step sequence number. Unlike CreateTaskBidiStream, it does not accept client inputs -- use it to observe or replay a task (the trajectory is not stored on the Task resource). This is the entry point for *observers*: it tails the trajectory from interaction storage without restoring the runtime actor, so it adds no restore latency and continues to follow output that a participant driving the task on a CreateTaskBidiStream connection produces. Observe-only clients should prefer this over a subscribe_task connect on CreateTaskBidiStream, which restores the actor and is meant for clients that will send input. |

| `upload_artifact_projects_locations_agents_tasks` | `POST /v1beta1/{task+}:uploadArtifact` | Uploads a file into a task's workspace. A client-streaming RPC: the request body is the file's raw bytes, relayed to storage a chunk at a time, so neither ESF nor this server holds the whole file. A broken connection restarts the upload from zero. `path` must be an absolute path under `/workspace/userdata/`. The task must not be in a terminal state. An existing file at `path` is replaced. At most 1 GiB. |
| `upload_projects_locations_agents_tasks` | `POST /v1beta1/{name+}:upload` | Uploads a file. The file is sent as media: post it to this method's URL with an `/upload` prefix, e.g. `/upload/v1beta1/{name}:upload`, carrying `path` as request metadata and the file's bytes as the media content. The task must not be in a terminal state, and an existing file at `path` is replaced. At most 10 GiB. |

If the server does not support this method, it returns `google.rpc.Code.UNIMPLEMENTED`. Note that this method is on a best-effort basis. It may return the latest state before the specified timeout (including immediately), meaning even an immediate response is no guarantee that the operation is done. |
